# Session Handoff

## Live Bugfix: premium 3D direct route loading time

- Date: 2026-06-30 HKT
- Agent ID: A06
- Workstream: Visualization Lab runtime / premium 3D direct routes
- Objective: Fix the reported slow/not-loading 3D lab route `https://mais.hk/student/tools/visualizations/us-ca-math-s4-chapter-05?grade=S4&track=all`.
- Initial evidence:
  - Production HTTP shell returned `200` in about `2.45s`; the failure boundary is client-side loading, not server route availability.
  - Production browser smoke reached DOMContentLoaded in about `5.7s` and first canvas in about `11.8s` on this machine.
  - The direct route loaded a `38.5MB` uncompressed JS chunk containing generated question-bank data, including unrelated Mainland HJB primary question records.
  - Root cause hypothesis: the direct premium 3D route imports `data/visualizationLabs.ts`, which imports aggregate `data/topics.ts`, which imports regional topic modules that import generated question/lesson packs. The reported California S4 route therefore pays for unrelated generated content before the 3D canvas appears.
- Plan:
  - Keep existing full Visualization Lab directory behavior intact.
  - Add a lightweight direct premium 3D metadata path for the reported California S4 probability lab.
  - Let `ConfiguredVisualizationLab` accept an already-resolved lab object so the optimized direct route does not statically import the full visualization catalog.
  - Add/adjust source boundary tests, then run focused tests, type-check if practical, and a local browser smoke for the reported route.
- Intended files:
  - `app/student/tools/visualizations/[labId]/page.tsx`
  - `components/visualizations/ConfiguredVisualizationLab.tsx`
  - `components/visualizations/VisualizationLabPage.tsx`
  - new A06-owned direct-route helper/component under `components/visualizations/`
  - focused A06 source tests under `components/visualizations/`
- Scope confirmation: A06-owned Visualization Lab files only; no secrets, no staging/commit/branch/push/deploy, and no unrelated dirty-tree reverts.
- Final changes:
  - Added a lightweight premium 3D direct route path for `us-ca-math-s4-chapter-05` and generic premium 3D metadata fallback for other generated premium topic paths.
  - Split `ConfiguredVisualizationLab` into a legacy catalog-resolving wrapper and `ConfiguredVisualizationLabDirect`, so direct topic pages can hydrate from the supplied lab object without invoking the full catalog fallback.
  - Removed the eager `data/visualizationLabs` preload from `VisualizationLabRouteShell`; navbar or route prefetches of `/student/tools/visualizations` no longer pull the `1797` catalog chunk.
  - Changed the direct route's Full directory control to click-only navigation, preserving access without exposing an eager directory href on first paint.
  - Added bundle-boundary coverage for the direct route, direct shell, direct configured lab export, and no route-shell catalog preload.
- Verification evidence:
  - Focused A06 tests passed `20/20`: `npx tsx --test components/visualizations/visualizationBundleBoundaries.test.ts components/visualizations/configuredVisualizationLabRegressions.test.ts`.
  - Isolated A22 production build passed with `NEXT_DIST_DIR=.tmp/a06-3d-lab-next NEXT_TSCONFIG_PATH=tsconfig.next.json NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build`; reported route size `/student/tools/visualizations/[labId]` was `4.14 kB`, first-load JS `206 kB`.
  - Isolated artifact scan on `.tmp/a06-3d-lab-next/static/chunks/app/student/tools/visualizations/[labId]/page-cb978aaf734686fe.js` found no `1797`, `data_visualizationLabs`, `visualizationLabs`, `VisualizationLabRouteShell`, generated-content IDs, or eager catalog preload patterns.
  - Isolated local production browser trace on `http://localhost:3132/student/tools/visualizations/us-ca-math-s4-chapter-05?grade=S4&track=all`: `catalogRequests=[]`, no large requests, direct marker at about `156ms`, 3D progressive surface/canvas ready at about `8.37s`, `data-viz-three-ready="true"`.
  - Root `npm run type-check` is currently blocked by unrelated generated `tmp/` and `var/` Next validator files referencing missing `app/student/lessons/page.js`; this was not introduced by the A06 source files and the isolated Next build's lint/type gate passed.
- Remaining release notes:
  - No deploy was performed; `mais.hk` will need an A22 clean release from an approved clean source/slice.
  - Shared `.next` browser smoke was not used as final evidence because an existing MAIS `next dev -p 3001` process rewrites shared `.next`; final proof used an isolated `NEXT_DIST_DIR`.
